Khyndiat Shaphang U Nongthoh 


U Rev. Richard Wurmbrand u dei u Pastor ha ri 
Rumania ha Europe ka ba long ka ri Komunist, u la 
shah set hapoh phatok bad u la shah pynshitom bein 
kumba khadsaw snem ha ka ri jong u hi na ka bynta U 
Jisu Khrist. U long uwei na ki nongtalam baradbah 
bad bakhraw jong ka Balang Khristan bad u long ruh u 
nongthoh kot bad u nonghikai uba khraw ha Rumania. 

Ka ka snem 1945, haba ki Komunist ki la ioh knieh 
ban synshar ha Rumania bad ki la pyrshang ban bat bor 
lut la ki nngmane baroh na ka bynta ka jingthmu jong 
ki, u Pastor Richard Wurmbrand shisyndon u la sdang 
ban ai ka jingsfaakri kaba khlain bad kaba donbot shibtin

napoh bad naba rich ia kt bricw jong u kiba la shah tch 
mraw bad ia ki shipai Russia kiba wan hiar thma ha 
kata ka por. Kunta mar mar la kem bad la set phatok 
ia u ha ki snem 1948, ryngkat bad ki lok joig 
Sabina. Ka lok jong u ki la loig ka noigtrei saja ted 
shitom la kumba lai snem- U Richard Wurmbtand u 
la do.i lai snem hapoh phatok marwei bad ym don mano 
mano ruh em lait noh sa tang kita ki nongpynshitotn 
bcin jong u. Hadien lai snen la pynkynriah noh ia u 
sha kawei pat ki phatok kaba bun briew kumba san 
snem ha kaba ki jingpynshiton bcin ki dang laid hi 
shaii shi shaid. 

Na ki bynta ki nam joig u kiba phnang kyllcng 
sawdong kum u rangbah Khristan, ki rangbah nonglam 
keien jong ki rinoigwei ha Rumania ki la pan 1^ ^ 
ka Sorkar Rumania ia ka ban pyllait noh ia u. Hynrci 
la iathuh ia ki ba u la phet noh na Rumania. Ki shipai 
barieh (Secret Police) kiba shu leh mynleh kum ki 
koidi kiba la pyllait, ki la iathuh ia ki lok jong u ba 
ki la leit on tep la u hapoh phatok ha ka jaka tep la ki 
koidi. la kiba ha iing ha sem jong u ha Rumania bad 
la ki paralok jong u kiba sha ki rl nongwei la iathuh ba 
ki dei ban klet noh ia u namar ba u U lah iap noh. 

Hadien phra snem la pyllait noh ia u bad u la sdang 
ban iatrei biang bad ki Balang Barieh (Underground 
Church). Ar snem hadien, kata ha ka snem 1959, la 
kem biang ia u bad la rai bi dei ban set ia u hapoh 
phatok arphew san snem kvnthih. 

La pyllait biang ia u ha ki snem 1964 ha kaba ka 
Sorkar ka la pynbna ia ka jingmap bad jingpyllait non

ia ki koidi baroh, bad sa shisienu la bteng ban iai tr^ 
ia ka kam jong ka jingshakri jong u ha kaba 
(underground ministry). Da kaba iohi ia ka jingma kaba 
khraw kaba lah ban jia na ka jingshah kem ha kaba lai 
sicn. ki Khristan kiba don ha ka ri Norway ki la ia kren 
bad ki bor synshar jong ki Komunist ia ka ban pyllait 
noh ia u na Rumania, Ka Sorkar Komunist ka la sdang 
ban die ia ki koidi baroh kiba shah set na ka bynta ki 
kam sain pyrthei. Ka dor jong uwei uwei u koidi ka

long £ 800 nc Rs. 14,000. Hynrei ka dor jong u pat ka 
long £ 2,500 ne Rs. 45,000. Ha u bnai May, 1966 u la 
phla ha Washington, America ha khmat ka U. S 
Senates Internal Security Sub-Committee (Ka Sobkoroiti 
kaba jadei bad ka kam poh iing jong ka Dorbar Thawain 
ba kham ha khlieh jong ka ri America) bad u la loit ia 
ki jam haduh syngkai ban pyni ia ki kbadphra tylli ki 
jingmong kiba jur na ki jingshah pynshitom bein kiba Ja 
tap ia ka met jong u. la ka jingiathuh khana sbaphang 
jong u la rah kylleng kylleng ha ki kot khubor ha 
America, Europe bad Asia. La mabam ia u ha u bnai 
September, 1966 ba ka Sorkar Komunist ha Rumania ka 
la rai ba dei ban phah pyniap noh ia u. Pynban um 
shym la sngap noh jar wat hapdeng kine ki jingbyrngem 
jong ka jingiap. la u la khot ba u dei ‘Ka Sur Jong Ka 
Balang Barich’* (The Voice of the Underground Church). 
Ki nongialam Khristan ki khot ia u “U Martar uba dang 
im” (a living martyr) bad “U Paul Jong ki ri Komunist* 
(The Iron Curtain Paul).

KA TIENKHMAT 


Di U Rev. H. M. Rapthap, B. D., M. Th., 
Principal, 

Cherra Theological College, Cherrapunji. 


Ka jingcrei barieh jong U Mynsietn U Blei ha ki ri 
Komunist ka la ju shon jur ia ka jingsngcw bad jingpyr- 
khat jong ng^. Ka pynkynroaw ia ka por jong ki Apostol 
bad ia ka Balang Khristan ha ki sngi kiba dang lung jong 
ka. Ki don napdcng jong ki Khristan ha kito ki ri kiba 
hap ban sakhi ia la U Trai haduh ka jingiap hi bad ki 
kylla long ki martar jong ka Balang. Ki don kiwei pat, 
ha ka jingpynind bastad jong U Blei, kiba kum u Pastor 
Richard Wurmbrand kiba la lait na ka jingiap wat haba 
ki la dei ban shah ia ki jingpynshitom bein kiba sngew- 
shyrkhei. 

Nga la pule ha ka Pharcng ia ki kit to katne tylli 
ki koc ba la thoh da u Pastor Richard Wurmbrand. Ki 
long kiba kordor shibun eh, Nga sngewbha ba I Mr. 
B. C. Lyngloh I la lah ban pynwan sha ka Khasi ia kme 
kawei ba la ai kyrteng ha ka Phareng “Tortured For 
Christ”. Ngim artatien ba kan long ka jingmyntoi kaba 
khraw ia kito baroh kiba ioh ban pule ia ka. Ha kane 
ka kitab ngi iohi ia ki bor jong ka Gospel U Khrist 
kiba lah ban pynkylla wat ia ki dohnud kiba eh tarn

lyngba ki jingsakhi jong ki riewngeit kiba shisha. Kan 
long kham tarn eh ka jingiarap ban sngewthuh ia ka dor 
jong ka jinglong synran bad ia ka kamram jong kito kiba 
la long Khristan ban sakhi ia la U jong U Trai da kaba 
iaineh skhem ha ka jingngeit haduh kiba kut. 


Cherrapunji 
The 1st November, 1971 


6 


H. M. Rapthap

ki Komunist. Nga la ai ha u nongpcit pyrkhing jorg ki

jong ki paitbah’ (Religion is the opium cf the people) 
lane kiwei ki wei pat ki jaid kyrteng kiba ia syriem ia kane. 
U nang tharai ia ki ba ki dei ki kot Komunist bad u la

Ka Balang Barieh ka long tang shiteng shiliang kaba

Ki Komunist, d i kaba iohi ia ka dur u Karl Marx, ki la 
iakniehparamaki ban thiedia ki kot. Ha ka por ba ki la 
poi haduh ka sla kaba shiphew bad ba ki la shem ba ka 
la long baroh tang shaphang U Blei bad U Jisu, ngi la 
don sha jngai bah na ki. 

Ka jingialap ha kine ki rukom kiba thymmai kam 
long satia kaba jem. Ki briew jong ngi ki la shah ban 
bein haduh katta katta, Ki Komunist ki la knieh noh
